# Consolidated changes — SP114_CW_156-5

Source authority: the supplied scan images. C and H are draft witnesses; their agreement is not treated as evidence. Each locus is counted once in the total and may count against both witnesses. Macro-only differences and certainty-only resolutions are excluded from substantive-change totals; crop-supported certainty decisions are recorded separately. Pixel boxes refer to the original whole-page JPG; coordinates use the top-left origin.

## Consequential corrections and retained slips

- Scan 26, 026-10, line below diagram, q formula: q=\varphi\unsure{\circ}p — The small loop between the large phi and the descending p is connected to the rising entry of p. A detached composition circle cannot be established. The formula remains marked rather than silently choosing either witness.
- Scan 26, 026-17, slanted note, sixth line, relation sign: \preceq — The upper angle and its lower arm are bent, and the separate lower bar is bowed. The straight ≤ in the displayed F≤G′≼G provides a same-page contrast. Resolve this particular sign as preceq; the whole marginal formula was also inspected.
- Scan 26, 026-28, item a), final letter after de: F — The glyph has a narrow upright with a short top arm and a projecting middle bar, unlike the broad open round G′ immediately to its left. It agrees in form with F elsewhere on the page. Retain the apparent authorial slip rather than repair it from the displayed relation.
- Scan 26, 026-45, bottom display, first support in first line: x\in|F| — The first support’s letter has a tall straight stem and two short horizontal arms. It is F, not the broad open G elsewhere on the sheet. The first support in the second-line index repeats F.
- Scan 26, 026-46, bottom display, end at right edge: \unsure{{}_{K}|\}} — After the tall bars enclosing a clear F, the ink drops down at the paper edge. The lowered cluster has a stem and two diverging arms, K-like, and might instead belong to a closing brace. There is no separately readable set-difference slash followed by G. Replace that conjectural extension by the blind lowered-K/brace alternative, without supplying a certain closing delimiter.
